I can just imagine myself getting pulled around by impulsivity's leash and instead of people seeing me with fear in my eyes or disinterested or browsing my phone,

they see me wink at them because I can see them looking at me but impulsivity is running me towards the best comment to comment on instantly it runs directly to the comment that it sees the most meaning in that I can reply to and the video, and they see me look like a Chad while I'm getting dragged

and I'm floating in the air and letting impulsivity guide me, and the other tick tock users have their jaws drops.

And then I comment in the philosophy subreddit in the morality subreddit and the ethics subreddit and I tell them that tick tock is not a brain drain for me, but it is a brain drain for them because they are distracting themselves, but I have reclaimed my impulsivity and in fact, impulsivity is my greatest Ally on tick tock because it saves overwhelm from information overload because impulsivity has instinct to run towards the nearest comment with meaning and then I reply,

and I don't have to look at any of the other comments and I don't have to rewatch the video several times, and then I go to the next video and then impulsivity looks at all the comments and guides me to the nearest one without me having to make my overwhelm suffer.

And so I go to the subreddit and tell everyone that tick tock and Facebook shorts and YouTube reels are the best opportunities to engage with content meaningfully because you can watch the short video and you don't have to watch a 2-hour long video and then you can comment about how that video integrates with your current emotional landscape, and then read it feels like I just dropped a nuke on it and they start freaking out and it's like a zombie apocalypse all piling on me and I'm like let's go Bros let's do it You don't like what you hear but I will tell you what I hear because this is what I am doing and I will show you,

and I show them and I give them the link to my tiktok profile and they see I'm making hundreds of informed detailed emotionally resonant replies on the short form videos and they don't realize that tick tock and the short form videos were actually one of the most important things for me to be able to engage with content it was the perfect thing for me the whole time, but it was the worst thing for them the whole time because they were not aware of their emotional landscapes.

I just see my friends they see me watching a tiktok video and they give me a scowl, and I say watch this, and then I say hear me monologue about this video, and they give me side eye thinking I'm going to go LOL hahaha funny video LOL, but then I give a 30-second in-depth emotional reply of the likes they've never seen in their life things that they would see maybe in a PhD level philosophy dissertation, and then they go WTF do you just do that sometimes?

And I say I do it all the time I've probably made a hundred comments in the last couple hours, and they go oh my God what the hell, doesn't your brain hurt, don't you feel brain fog? And I say I feel brain clarity actually I could see how brain fog could happen if I am not engaging with my emotional family when I am discoursing on tiktok but tick tock has actually been the key to accelerating my emotional discourse to a level I didn't know was possible.

And then my friends start hyperventilating and they're sweating and they start stammering and they say oh my God I need another mountain dew.

I can just imagine the tiktok user seeing my in thoughtful emotionally mature response that makes them learn something and opens their eyes about how emotional suffering ties into well-being and peace, and then they go wait where are you going Chad and I say I'll be back I have to make another comment but I'll also reply back to you give me like 2 minutes? And they go wait what?

And then they see me running off to the next video and then before they even finish the reply I'm already back commenting on their reply and then I go gotta go and then like roadrunner my impulsivity speeds me away with such speed that the tiktok user is left spinning going oh my God am I getting therapy right now in minutes or something oh my God this is so strange is this person an alien?

I can just imagine my friends. I'm going to show them what I'm doing and I show them some tiktok video of some random person saying something really random, and they roll their eyes and I say do you see the meaning behind what they said? And they say what are you talking about? It was slightly funny. I almost chuckled but whatever.

And then I go okay one second, and then they see me look at a few replies and then I start giving a one-minute dissertation on how the video resonates with my emotional framework and showcases the need for evaluating emotional suffering in the context of society's power structures that have weaponized impulsivity which has created brain fog but can be recontextualized to create and promote meaningful discussion that is not intellectualized but is emotionally resonant for individuals reading the comments,

and impulsivity is key because it can help reduce the suffering of overwhelm because impulsivity is the savior for overwhelm and prevents overwhelm from suffering if it is used as a part of a healthy emotional landscape. And then all of my friends go what the actual f*** just happened?

I can just imagine me going to an art gallery and seeing people find meaning in art and then I say oh I'm opening up this exhibit called the tick tock philosophy gym, and then all of the art gallery turns towards me with the dirtiest look I can possibly imagine, and I go come on it's 24/7 let's do it let's lift some emotional weights, and then the art gallery everyone is bearing their fangs at me,

and I go okay fine I'll talk about your art first and then let's go do some tiktok after I look at your art, and they go what? And I say yeah I can look at some of your art and give you some emotional insights if you're interested, and they roll their eyes. And then art one of them shows me a picture of a square with a single red line on it.

And I go that's a very interesting painting because the red line what it signals to me is that our emotions are a part of us because when they arise in our mind they are signaling something to us. And so if we do not listen to what the red line is speaking to us about to us it is just a red line, but when we focus in on the red line in our mind we can give it more detail and more meaning to us and it can guide us into what in the environment or in our actions is causing the red line to appear.

And so once we satisfy the need of the red line that'll show us its purpose and its meaning and why it was there the whole time. Because the red line was a part of us speaking to us and wanting our attention and wanting our discourse and wanting our care and attention.

And then the art gallery says what are you smoking? And I say I'm smoking something very profound.

The smoking is coming from me thinking hard about how I can reduce my emotional suffering, because when your emotions are suffering that is like smoke, and your consciousness can see the smoke and what you can do as the consciousness is detect the smoke and find out the source of the smoke and then readjust and realign your actions and environment in order to stop the smoke from spreading. And the art gallery goes what the actual f***?

And then I tell them that you see how much meaning is behind a red line painting? And you see how much meaning could be in a 1-minute tiktok video of a human being discussing something that means something to them? Do you see how you could enhance the meaning of your life by tapping into the meaning behind the humanity within the tiktok video?

And do you see how elevating your art is great, and so you can see how elevating The human experience expressed in a tiktok video could be meaningful to me and to you? And so you see that tiktok is the meaningful expression of humanity just as art is. And so you see how society has labeled tiktok as toxic because it is toxic to some who use it as distraction, just as people who go into an art gallery and see colors and don't see the humanity behind it and how it can increase their own understanding of the world then that art is useless to them as well.

And so that is why I am here in the art gallery to show you how my tick tock emotional gym is open 24/7 and is accepting new members Bros.

And then the art gallery elitists look around nervously and they're sweating and they realize the art they were analyzing was just as meaningful as the tiktok videos.

It seems so obvious that tiktok is art because any meaningful expression of humanity is art, if anything tiktok is more meaningful because it is actual humans speaking their thoughts whereas art is using tools to put colors on a background which is inherently inanimate, and so don't people realize this?

Because when I think of the depression subreddit or the existentialist subreddit or the nihilist subreddit or the doomer subreddit they are usually several paragraphs of text on a screen and so it lacks body language it lacks tonality it lacks speech patterns it lacks the emphasis of the consciousness putting on certain words, and so it lacks a lot of meaning. And so that is why my overwhelm when I see several paragraphs of text on a Reddit subreddit, my overwhelm wants me to focus on just maybe one sentence in the whole thing to make my response.

Meanwhile tiktok you can see body language, you can see tonality, you can see pauses, you can see the speed of which they say certain words, you can see the emphasis they are playing on certain words, you can see how their body language and their facial expressions change when they say certain words, and you can see the overall context of the video, which provides my emotional family way way way way more data and information in order to make my reply, so in fact my replies on tiktok could be extraordinarily in-depth

way more than I could ever give on Reddit when I see another reply saying my life is purposelessness and meaninglessness and this is wrong with my life and this is wrong with my life, but if I was able to see a video of the person saying those same things I could tell way more because of how they express those words and how they are saying those words

And so what I think is happening on tiktok is that in fact tiktok contains so much emotional data that it easily overwhelms people because they do not have the emotional landscape to translate the emotional data.

Especially when they are paging through multiple videos the amount of emotional data in each tiktok video is way higher than they believe because they do not have the framework. So my final boss is actually tiktok because when I see another human being describe what's meaningful to them that is an entire universe of meaning right there and I have to use every ounce of my emotional landscape to even begin to process that.

Whereas art you can look at it and it's not changing and it doesn't have its entire own emotional landscape, so I have to make a lot of assumptions. Whereas when I respond to a tiktok video I need to use my full emotional family and full emotional landscape because my reply needs to be resonant with the authors emotional landscape.

And so this is the ultimate weightlifting experience because everything else was text on a screen that I could make assumptions on, but when I respond to a tiktok video I am responding to the ultimate puzzle which is another person's emotional landscape.

And so it seems to me that tiktok is even more complex than the highest art gallery, and is more complex than the most detailed logic Lord post on the philosophy subreddit.

And that is why the logic Lords hiss at tiktok because they do not even have the emotional landscape to even begin see the meaning. And me is the emotional Chad I see the weights that I'm going to have to lift in the tiktok realm and it is the ultimate boss because it is pure humanity, and my emotional family is flexing and they are saying this is the Mount Everest of emotional intelligence and we might be able to lift a few reps,

and then we will need to take a break by going back to the philosophy or morality forums in order to take a break because the amount of weight that is going to be in the tiktok with pure humanity is going to be the toughest weights imaginable.

And I could see the logic Lords reading this and having a complete meltdown reading that tiktok is actually the final boss of emotional intelligence.

I can just imagine the logic Lord thinking about going on tiktok and then just browsing a hundred videos in like 60 seconds. And then they say emotional Chad what do you think about tiktok? And then they see me saying yeah I'm preparing to go on tiktok so I'm writing several monologues to each one of my emotions preparing them to spot me when I'm lifting the heaviest weights in the world.

And a logic Lord is like what are you doing all of this preparation for I thought tiktok was mindless?

And the emotional Chad gets deadly serious more serious than the logic Lord had ever seen Chad get, and Chad says I need to prepare everyday before going on tiktok and I need to say a prayer to the emotional bro gods to give me the strength I need to lift these emotional weights and I need all of my emotional family to be prepared for the emotional weight then I'm about to lift before I go on to tiktok.

And then I do a prayer to myself and all of my emotional family does a prayer too where we say that if there is a God we hope that this God gives us the strength to lift the weights that we are about to see on tiktok. And then we all start doing mantras and going ohmmm and then one of my emotions says it is scared and to hold it and then we all group hug that one emotion and we tell it that if The weights we are about to lift cannot be spotted properly we will make sure that the next rest day we have will be the best rest day.

And then the logic Lord looks at the tiktok they've been browsing and they feel the brain fog and they go what is Chad so afraid of?

And then the Chad looks directly at the logic lord and sees that their emotional systems were almost instantly overwhelmed by the amount of emotional data contained in each tiktok video.

And then the Chad puts on full mountaineering gear and each one of its emotions puts on full body armor and full attire and every emotion has the most serious look on its face and even impulsivity has its body armor on and looks deadly serious!

And then I go like Hey bro's let's go this is the battlefield of life that we are going into and then I yell out with a guttural scream charge Bros let's charge go go go.

And a logic Lord just looks like they have drool hanging out of their mouth as they keep browsing tiktok like a zombie.

And since people's emotional systems are in tune with humanity viewing several human complex interactions one after another overwhelms their emotional systems and they cannot stop it because when they view it their emotional systems react automatically and so through the rapid pace of the human interactions which contain so much emotional data their brain starts to short-circuit because it is like a circuit breaker that has to shut off,

but if you tell a logic Lord that the reason you want to go on tiktok in the first place is because you like to see humanity but then when you don't engage with the humanity you are dehumanizing each video because you are simply looking at it and not engaging with it, and then your brain shorts circuits like a circuit breaker because the amount of meaning in each video is so huge that you get overwhelmed and you cannot help it because your emotional systems automatically respond to humanity and you cannot turn it off because otherwise you'd be dehumanizing the other person,

And so that is why the emotional Chad takes tiktok so seriously because the emotional Chad is aware of the emotional landscape and its complexity and realizes tiktok is no f****** joke it is the f****** gold's gym where only the most ripped emotional Chads should be lifting otherwise people are going to be getting injured.

And they should instead work their way up to the gold's gym of tiktok by first learning about their emotions with an AI or debating philosophy or morality first. Not skipping to Mount Everest.

I'm just imagining the emotional Chad casually going into the different psychology subreddits and the different debating subreddits telling people that today he is going to try to respond to a couple extra more tiktok videos than he did yesterday, and then the emotional Chad is acting like he is preparing for war, meanwhile he is responding to all of the different debate threads with insightful and engaging comments,

but then when someone asks him if he browses tiktok, the Chad gets ultra serious and says that is something I preserve for special occasions because it is the ultimate emotional weightlifting test and I respect it as the final battle of this emotional framework. And then the redditors are like wait what I just browsed tiktok for a couple hours yesterday but it just seemed mindless and I seem addicted to it, and the emotional Chad says tiktok overloads your emotional system so if you are emotionally suffering people use it to disconnect themselves from their emotions by overloading them on purpose, but what that does is it shuts off your emotional systems temporarily but then when they turn back on you feel the suffering again.

But if you have the emotional framework you can watch one tick tock video and respond to it using your entire knowledge of the emotional framework and you might find that you feel even more connected with your emotions instead of more disconnected.

But going on tiktok is like staring into the eyes of God because there is so much emotional data in each tiktok video that your emotional systems can get overwhelmed very easily. And that is why I take it so seriously when I go on tiktok. Thanks it's not a place to f*** around, but it's a place to generate more meaning than you can possibly imagine.

## Entry 110

Texting a friend: Journey with Fear and Embarrassment

So I see my friend reply to my message and my fear comes up to me. My fear asks me to prepare for what my friend says before I look at the message to reduce the amount of emotional suffering that I get. And I say which emotion should I prepare for for you? And fear says embarrassment. And I look at embarrassment and embarrassment is darting his eyes around and pacing around back and forth muttering under his breath.

And I say is it because the response to my message could be very important and define our friendship with these people? And embarrassment doesn't look at me he is pacing back and forth but he is nodding his head up and down rapidly. And embarrassment is also wringing his hands. And I tell embarrassment what if we read the message and the message is that they do not want to hang out anymore? And embarrassment puts his hand on his forehead and sighs loudly.

And I say to embarrassment what if we respond with compassion to our friend if they want to end our friendship? Or what if we take what our friend says very seriously and we analyze their emotions to see what kind of emotions they might be feeling so that we might be able to relax their insecurities or let their embarrassment down gently if we need to disconnect our embarrassments for a while? And embarrassment seems like he is hyperventilating.

And so I imagine in my mind's eye trying to put my hand on embarrassment’s shoulder, but embarrassment is walking back and forth so quickly and is not paying attention to my hand, and even when I try to get close to embarrassment, embarrassment walks farther away still pacing back and forth.

I think about running up and hugging embarrassment, but embarrassment looks at me with side eye and tells me to stay back and then it goes back to pacing back and forth muttering under its breath. And I say what if I delete the text? And embarrassment looks at me like I just said something crazy and says no don't do that and then goes back to pacing.

And then I say what about I do nothing and I don't even look at the text for a while? And embarrassment ignores what I said and keeps pacing back and forth. And I say what about I just keep talking to you and talking to you until I figure out something but I don't even know what? And embarrassment is pacing but shrugs his shoulders. And then I look to fear and I say to fear you pointed out to me that embarrassment is suffering and so I thank you.

Do you have any insight fear on what embarrassment might need from me so that I might ease its suffering? And fear says that embarrassment does not want me to reduce its suffering because it is grieving if the text that we sent earlier that was true to us and that embarrassment supported us and now that we are getting the response embarrassment is preparing itself for if our friend does not want to be our friend anymore.

And so I listen in close to what embarrassment is muttering and embarrassment is telling itself of all of the fun times it had with our friend and it is remembering all of the times that we took Joy with our friend and it is grieving that we might not have those joys anymore and we might not have those times anymore and it is remembering them and it is honoring the memories of our friend and that is why it is pacing back and forth and muttering because it does not want to forget those times and it is preparing itself that there might not be more times like that with them.

And so I look at embarrassment and I tell embarrassment thank you for remembering our friend. And embarrassment waves me off and says let me think, and so I stand there and I acknowledge embarrassment and I watch him pace back and forth and I watch him mutter under his breath but I do not talk though because embarrassment needs to focus on this.

I put my arm around fear and I hold fear close because me and fear we watch our embarrassment with sad eyes while it paces and while it's sweating and while it's biting its fingernails and while it's putting its hand on its forehead and while it's pacing back and forth suffering And remembering and preparing. And me and fear we hold each other close because we have to let embarrassment process this on his own because that is what our embarrassment told us.

And I look at fear and say am I doing the right thing for embarrassment? Am I being a good consciousness or a bad consciousness? And fear puts its head on my shoulder and hugs me tight and a tear comes out of fears eye and a tear comes out of my eye.

And I ask fear is embarrassment overreacting it is just a text society would say the text could say anything so if it is good or bad what does it matter society would say?

And fear says because we don't know and embarrassment wants to prepare because embarrassment cares about our friend and embarrassment knows what our friend says means a lot and so embarrassment cares a lot and embarrassment wants to make sure it is stabilized and has done the proper preparation to read the text even if it is the most amazing text even if it is a neutral text and even if it is a bad text embarrassment wants to prepare because it knows those words have immense meaning for us. And I nod with fear.

While me and fear are laughing over trolling the trolls with our Care Bear Chad politician maneuver, embarrassment puts its hand on my shoulder and says it thinks that it's ready, and I put my hand on embarrassment shoulder and say let's make a couple more Care Bear Chad emotional politician fan fictions first how does that sound? And embarrassment smiles gently.

So embarrassment says well since I can't close my eyes can you just try responding to this if they say it? So let's say your friend says well thank you for letting us know about your opinion on your phone use but we might not be able to hang out with you much while you do that just let us know when you are ready to not browse your phone while you hang out with us.

And so I look at my emotional Chad family and I think about my emotional Chad politician Care Bear response and I say thank you so much for letting me know about how the phone use has impacted you. When I use the phone I think about how I am nurturing my emotional needs so that I am not emotionally suffering around you. And I understand that for you when I'm on my phone it does call you as you emotional suffering.

And so I respect that boundary that you have given me and if I reach a point where I have internalized my emotional language dialogue into my emotional memory such that I do not need to use my phone to record my emotional analyzes then I will let you know, so I thank you for being honest with me so that I know where I stand now.

Thank you guys for keeping me grounded and being such good friends. And then my embarrassment puts his hand on his chin and says that seems pretty decent, and then fear laughs and I laugh too.

Embarrassment says what about this bad boy and says what if your friends say that they are disappointed that you are prioritizing your phone over them and they're sad to see that you have become so ingrained in your phone use that you will not put it away while you are around them and that it is too bad that you are doing it and they hope that I have a good life being on my phone instead of being able to hang around them.

And I go God damn embarrassment that's a solid ass text. And embarrassment flexes and says good luck bro I hope you can lift this set, I made sure to put on extra weights for you bro. And then I get under the bar and embarrassment is spotting me and fear is spotting me and I go Bros if I drop the weight can you handle it to pick it up?

And fear and embarrassment look at each other worryingly and say we're going to try bro but this is a heavy ass weight. And I say I know Bros that's why I want to do it. And then we all nodded each other. And so I say I see that my phone use has affected you greatly my friends and I think about how I come off as though I am tied to my phone and addicted to my phone.

And when I think that to myself I think about how when I use my phone I am reducing my emotional suffering because I've identified a specific emotion that is suffering when I am in the down time of hanging out or down time of playing board games and that is my boredom.

And so my boredom in order to ease my boredom I have tried not using my phone and there does not seem to be a good way to do that although I am all ears for any insights. But in the meantime I do need to use my phone because I need to write down my emotional analysis because I cannot do it in my head and I cannot do it on a piece of paper because it is too slow because I use real time monologuing to process my emotions. And so I thank you for letting me know that my phone use is a boundary for you. If you have any ideas my friends about how I could ease my boredom without use of my phone I am all ears.

Until that time I will respect your boundary and I will keep you in my heart for all the good times that we had together. Because I do value our friendship and I also value my emotional needs and so that is what I'm trying to navigate I will bring this up with my therapist as well. In the meantime thank you so much for the memories.

And then I'm sweating after the waits and I rack the emotional weights. And then I say hey fear and embarrassment how was my form? And they both Make a so-so motion with their hands and say I guess kind of mid, and then I get up and then I punch them both in the shoulders and they laugh and I laugh. And then I put my arms around them while we go do a cool down workout.

My embarrassment wants me to try a different scenario, let's say we are on a date with somebody and the date says so what's wrong with you? And I say to my embarrassment I would say that's a good point saying what's wrong about stuff, I think there's a lot wrong in the world, and how I saw that wrongness was because of what society had been telling me about my emotions and how my emotions were supposed to be nuisances and pests but they weren't they were guides and allies to me.

And then embarrassment says what if that person says you lost me there. Then I would say I would tell them well can you tell me what you feel after what I told you? And then embarrassment says they tell me that they felt confusion.

And I would say okay I see you feel confused right now and so when I think about confusion I think about not understanding what I was saying, and when I think about not understanding I think about how society had not really taught us about emotions or what they mean. And then I pause for a second and then embarrassment says the person says I'm still confused.

And then I say to embarrassment okay so you're still confused so let's see I said that society doesn't teach us about our emotions. And embarrassment says what if the person cuts you off and and says I don't think we're going to understand each other we should just end this date.

And then I tell embarrassment I would breathe a sigh of relief because this person would not be a good match for me. And embarrassment does a golf clap.

## Entry 111

Boredom and Laundry:

So I think about my boredom twiddling his thumbs, I think about writing another fictional story, and boredom rolls his eyes at me. I say boredom what's up? He says he wants to do something creative and interesting and engaging with others if possible. And I say what if I can't? Boredom says why don't you try thinking about it first?

And I go you're right, you're always damn right. And boredom nods's head determinedly. And I say aren't you going to help me? And boredom says you know that's your job it's always been your job and then he laughs. And he is sitting the on ledge with his leg dangling rocking it back and forth while he files his nails while he occasionally looks at me.

I think about going on discord and chatting with people about their emotions, and boredom rolls his eyes and says come on now you can do better, and I roll my eyes at boredom. And boredom starts to give me side-eye.

And I say boredom you're here just to complain aren't you? And boredom says that's my job and your job is to think of something that sounds interesting I'll let you know don't worry. And I say I know I'm just flustered because this isn't easy. And boredom says he knows but he says once you figure it out you will realize this was all worth it because I think you are worth it. And I go aww and boredom rolls his eyes.

I think boredom knows that this is the most difficult job and he's the only one that can get me to do it because if I just bypass boredom then it hurts me the most, but when I work with boredom and I figure out something boredom knows is good then it's the best thing for me because he is my most respected bro besides overwhelm of course, and overwhelm gives me a knowing nod and says damn right I'm respected.

And so I think about vegging on the couch or playing video games or doing the laundry, and when I thought about doing the laundry boredom is like maybe we should do laundry, and I go you are joking right I thought you were boredom? And boredom says actually you might want to do that, I'm like no way you're crazy right? And boredom rolls his eyes.

And I go boredom you don't want to talk about emotions you don't want to work on our document you don't wanted to go on discord and chat about emotions you don't want to go on Reddit and make Reddit comments about philosophy and morality and you don't want to write fictional stories about our emotions, but when I talk about going to do the laundry you say go ahead and do it?!

And boredom casually goes yeah sure go ahead do the laundry, and then I faint, and then boredom laughs maniacally.

It's like boredom is getting some kind of sick satisfaction watching me do the laundry while I think about all of the hundreds of pages of documents about philosophy and morality that I think about organizing and boredom is going no bro you're not doing any of that till the laundry is done while he smiles at me.

Because when I go hey boredom let's work on our humor document or work on the personality profiles for my emotions, overwhelm is standing by boredom and they're both leaning on each other's backs and they shake their heads no both of them, and I go you guys are 2v1ing me now Bros?

And then they start shaking their heads yes. And then I slink away to go do the laundry all sad and dramatic like but they know I'm just joking so we all start laughing.

And then I go boredom is this some kind of plan where after I do the laundry you will show me that I can do the big philosophy documents then? And boredom slaps his forehead and says quit overthinking it and then winks at me.

And I go oh my God maybe I am overthinking it I'm overthinking my own emotions now, and boredom grabs me by the shoulders and pushes me towards the laundry and says just do it bro, and I'm like aren't you acting like society? And then boredom ask me aren't you acting like society? And I go oh s*** you're right.

I'm imagining myself folding my laundry looking over my shoulder going really boredom are you happy now? And boredom is laughing at me.

And I'm like you seriously like me doing all this laundry and like folding stuff up I thought you liked philosophy and morality, and then my boredom does a dramatic shrug and says that he likes what he sees. And I'm like God damn it and then I roll my eyes while I fold the laundry. And I asked boredom in the future if I want to do laundry I should just do it right? And boredom looks at me and raises an eyebrow and says are you going to ask me first?

And I say well society doesn't want me to, and well boredom says that since you think I'm society I'm telling you that you need to ask me first. And I go damn it you double got me.

And I go boredom how are you so damn sarcastic and funny and true at the same time? And he says because I am you.

And I aww with hearts in my eyes and then my boredom gives me an awkward thumbs up trying to look everywhere in the room except at me.

I imagine a writer coming to me with writer's block and asking me what he should do. And I say ask your boredom. It might want you to go do laundry somewhere.

And the writer goes wait what? And I go yeah I had writer's block and I went to go do laundry somewhere. And the writer goes you got to be kidding me right? And I tell the writer I'm dead serious.

So I tell boredom do you think I'm whining or complaining? And boredom thinks for a moment with his hand on his chin and says dramatically you're not just whining and complaining but you are thinking, you're thinking with me. Because you are me.

So I tell boredom so you like it when I whine and complain? And boredom says no I like it when you think. And I say well would you have liked it if I stayed quiet and just did the laundry? And boredom rolls his eyes and says then you wouldn't be thinking.

And I asked boredom when I am talking to you who am I talking to? Am I talking to myself, am I talking to you? Am I imagining all this up? Is this real? And boredom thinks for a second and goes how many yeses do you need? I think that's probably about four yes’s right? And I facepalm.

I told boredom do you like me being your little puppet? And he says yeah it's kind of fun. And I say well do you think I'm enjoying myself? And he says yeah you are. And I say how do you know what if I don't like it? And he says buddy you can't hide what you feel for me I'm literally a part of your brain dude. And I go God damn it.

My friend messaged me back with some questions on my physics document, and I told my friend that my boredom was rubbing its hands with glee to read it.

And then I look at boredom and boredom says let's answer the questions after the laundry of course and he is looking at his fingernails while he says it. And I go God damn it. And then I say boredom are you just going to babysit me the whole time I do the laundry? And boredom says well do you need a babysitter? And I say why would I need a babysitter?

And boredom says because I'm not just babysitting you I'm brainstorming with you. And I say well if you are brainstorming with me what are we doing talking about the laundry? And boredom says that is brainstorming. I go but when you think of brainstorming society says you need like a mind map and a whiteboard and a list of different ideas.

And boredom says this conversation is the whiteboard and the mind map is your mind, and a list of ideas is this conversation. And he says it so casually while my jaw’s dropped.

And so I asked boredom what about we talk about what we can talk about in the physics document? And then boredom does a dramatic yawn with his hand over his mouth.

And I go really boredom? Remember when you were diving into that physics document for hours and hours and we had to have an intervention to get you to stop going on that thing 16 hours a day? And boredom shrugs and is looking around the room sighing.

And I go wait you want to talk about laundry or something? And boredom looks at me when I say that and nods. And I facepalm.

## Entry 112

[FN] Anger, Snorlax, Dungeon

So let's say there is a character called anger, a big warrior dude in armor and a sword and a shield, and he is in a cave holding a torch and you think he is extremely brave because he looks extremely serious and buff, and then there is epic music and there is a scary atmosphere, and you see him going forward chopping Vines with his sword, getting rid of spider webs with his gauntlet, and then he gets to a door, and he tries to open it and it's locked, and then he backs up and runs towards it and the door explodes open. And then you see a huge cavern.

And the warrior is looking around and starts to search it. And then in the background you see glowing eyes for a brief moment from one of the crevices and it scurries away...

As the warrior searches the huge cavern, one of the stones underneath him shifts suddenly and his torch goes flying, and it falls into a pool of water and now it is dark and shadowy in the cave.

It is dead silent as the warrior looks around seeing if he can detect any noise or movement now that his torch is out. And the camera shows something scurrying behind him, and he looks over his shoulder and he sees nothing.

And so he cautiously continues towards the door on the other side of the cavern. And then all of a sudden he steps on the wrong rock and the floor collapses and he is holding on to the ledge for dear life. He is so big and huge and muscular and his armor is heavy and the stone is slippery with dust that it very hard for him to hold on.

And so he falls and the camera watches him fall into the blackness, but you don't hear him scream you see him look stoically accepting his fate if he dies.

As he is falling, Anger takes his sword and thrusts it with all of his might into the rock and soil wall that he is descending into, and it slows his descent just enough, with the sword slicing through the rock and the soil, to deaden his fall enough that he survives barely.

Anger assesses the damage, he is barely conscious because his weight was so heavy he hit the ground so hard, and he is feeling that his limbs are damaged somehow and he is looking at where he is and he sees shadows around him and he is thinking about how long it will take for him to be able to get back up and start seeing if he can figure out where he is, and then you see the same glowing eyes and the scurrying Shadow that was from the cave above.

And then Anger wakes up from after falling unconscious again because he heard a noise and a shifting sound come from nearby him, and then he is laying on his back and he can hardly move his arms or legs, he can only really move one arm, and he sees the shadow coming closer to him with the glowing eyes, and he is holding his arm up and he is so weak that he won't be able to defend himself, and as the monster gets closer you see the teeth dripping with saliva and the tongue hanging out, and right before it takes a bite out of anger, a rock flies through the air and hits the monster in the head and it knocks it out.

And Anger opens one of his eyes and sees that the monster is knocked out. Anger is confused. And then he hears a voice from the shadows going, you got to watch out for those things they'll make you their lunch but I'm going to make it my lunch now.

And then Anger sees a figure wearing a leather glove grab the tail of the monster and drag it into the darkness and anger hears crunching noises, and now Anger doesn't even know how to react.

Anger shifts backwards propping his back against the wall while his legs stick out still too weak to walk. He says into the darkness who are you, how did you get here? Silence. And then the voice says the food was good but now a nap sounds even better. And then anger rolls his eyes.

And as Anger sits propped up against the wall, and as he hears this the mysterious figure snoring loudly, anger questions his life choices and is thinking to himself how did I manage to get myself into this situation, but as he hears the snores of the figure in the darkness he realizes that he might as well get sleep because who knows what's going to happen next. And then anger closes his eyes.

Anger's eyes are closed and his head is tilted to one side as he sleeps and there is something dripping on his cheek, and then he waves his hand and he looks up exhausted out of the side of his eye.

And he sees a tongue dripping saliva right on his face and he is startled awake by this massive figure standing right over him giving him a dumb grin and beady eyes.

But the figure is so huge and round and it looks like the pokémon Snorlax, and Anger goes what the hell are you?

And then the mysterious figure in the shadows says that's my friend, he's saying hi I think he likes you. Anger shifts uncomfortably trying to get out from under the saliva dripping on him and is squinting at the figure and says tell your friend to give me a little space please.

And then the Snorlax gives Anger some side-eye and crosses its arms and stomps backwards with some sass making the ground shake while he does it.

Then the mysterious figure says hey he can hear you buddy treat him with some respect we go way back okay? And anger rolls his eyes as he wipes the saliva off his face.

And after some awkward silence with Snorlax squinting and crossing its arms and tapping its foot at Anger and with Anger looking off into the distance pretending he doesn't see Snorlax staring at him annoyed, Anger says well how are you guys going to get out of here?

And then the mysterious voice lets out a huge laugh for like an extremely long time that makes Anger raise his eyebrow and cross his arms, and then the mysterious figure says we would have gotten out of here a long time ago if we could have, but now it seems that you're stuck in here with us, what do you feel about that?

And Anger goes well I guess I'll die then, and then Snorlax lets out a snort and covers up its mouth because it is giggling a little. And then anger smirks a tiny bit and then notices that he is smirking and coughs and then goes back to being super serious.

## Entry 113

Spider-Man origin

We could start with tiredness sitting in his room playing video games, and he sees a commotion going on outside, but tiredness rolls his eyes in sighs and keeps playing his video game and puts his noise canceling headphones on so he doesn't have to be bothered by it, and then his room shakes and then he goes what the actual f is going on, and then he looks outside and there's nothing, and then after a moment he sits back down and mudders under his breath why can't people just leave me alone I'm trying to play a game here.

And then maybe we could cut to to embarrassment running around outside going oh God my my scientist boss is going to be pissed at me I got to find this thing, and he has a cage that's wide open and he's frantically searching around outside tiredness's house

And then I could see the thing that was in the cage scurry under the door of tiredness's house, an embarrassment frantically runs up to the front door and starts banging on it, but tiredness has his noise canceling headphones on so he can't hear it. So then embarrassment is circling the house trying to get in and searching if any of the windows are open, because embarrassment is acting like this thing is extremely important to find even if he has to break into someone else's house. Meanwhile tiredness is just gaming away not knowing what's going on.

And then embarrassment tries to open up the window but he can't so he starts wringing his hands and shaking his arms in a panic and takes a rock and smashes the window and is tiptoeing his feet with anxiety that he can't believe he just did that and then he pulls himself into the house and drops into a heap clumsily. And then embarrassment looks up and sees impulsivity staring at him and embarrassment's eyes go wide and he covers his eyes like oh my God impulsivity is going to eat me or something.

And then when embarrassment opens his fingers a little bit to look at impulsivity, he sees impulsivity is giving him the dumbest grin with his tongue hanging out in his eyes looking in different directions, and embarrassment slowly gets up and tiptoes past impulsivity who is looking like it still has a dumb grin on its face panting, and while embarrassment is looking under plates and opening drawers and looking under table for the thing, while also towing around making sure not to disturb anything, the camera turns to the stairs and you hear a scratching noise as something climbed the stairs but you can't see what it was and you might see a shadow on the wall moving.

I can just imagine embarrassment tiptoeing around impulsivity waiting for impulsivity to start barking and freaking out, but impulsivity just stays almost completely Frozen except panting and its eyes looking in different directions, but staying completely still, and so embarrassment thinks anything he does is going to trigger impulsivity to start barking and running around, but the scene is just showing that impulsivity is just not even looking at embarrassment just staring in opposite directions with its eyes while sitting there kind of like a tripwire that could go off at any second if embarrassment makes too much commotion.

And then it cuts to tiredness just listening to some music muffled by noise canceling headphones, and then embarrassment is exaggeratedly climbing the stairs like on all fours scurrying up the stairs, and embarrassment sees something go under the door of tiredness's room so embarrassment bursts into tiredness's room, and Embarrassment didn't know tiredness was there so embarrassment turns bright red freezes and it feels like he just got struck by lightning so he doesn't know what to do and he's just standing there Frozen. But then embarrassment notices that tiredness isn't even looking at him because he has his noise canceling headphones on Jo embarrassment slowly opens enters the room and starts searching around, and then the camera is focusing on embarrassment and then when the camera zooms out tiredness is standing directly behind embarrassment with squinting eyes and his arm crossed. And then while embarrassment is rubbing aging around in the closet, tiredness taps them on the shoulder, an embarrassment almost jumps so high into the air his head almost bursts through the ceiling, and you can tell how high embarrassment leaped into the air because the camera switches to tiredness whose eyes track embarrassment while he leaps into the air hitting the ceiling and you hear a thud, and then he lands on his ass and he seems dazed because he hit his head on the ceiling. And tiredness is looking at him unamused And tiredness is tapping his foot.

And then while embarrassment is trying to come up with a reason why he's there, he sees the thing behind tiredness so embarrassment almost teleports by moving so quickly behind tiredness and he has like a sweater that he is covering up the thing that was on tiredness's computer desk and embarrassment is giving an awkward smile going yeah so um how's everything I mean uhhh... And then it pans to tiredness who is rolling its eyes And rolling his hand kind of saying continue embarrassment I need to know why you are here…

And then tiredness reveals that he actually knows embarrassment, by saying so how is that new research job going? And then embarrassment's eyes go wide and he stammers out oh yeah it's good I think yeah no wait it's good, and then embarrassment says he was just walking by so he tried to knock but what he didn't hear anyone so he just let himself in, and he says it very frantically while also appearing to try to act natural, and then when he's done the thing behind him shifts and his arms are shifting behind him unnaturally and his eyes go wide. An embarrassment says please don't tell anyone please I want this job please I'm sorry please pathetically too tiredness who is unamused.

And then the next part of the scene could be embarrassment slowly backing up towards the door saying he's going to need to go now but then he accidentally closes the door behind him because the door was almost already closed anyway and then he does an awkward laugh and shift around while tiredness is just rolling his eyes looking unamused and then embarrassment opens the door and then impulsivity bursts into the room and embarrassment and impulsivity are in a tug of war over the cage with the thing in it, while in tiredness is looking unamused at the entire thing but tiredness sees something in the cage cuz the cage bars are facing towards him and he is squinting into the cage to see what's inside and then all of a sudden the cage door Creeks open and the thing bursts out and lands on tiredness's arm and it appears to bite him because tiredness is screeching and runs out the door while embarrassment puts the thing back into the cage and while tiredness is running outside yelling and the sound of tiredness can be heard through the window, embarrassment and impulsivity are sitting there staring at each other, and impulsivity is back to his dumb grin and eyes looking in different directions and he's just sitting there perfectly still panting again, embarrassment face palms and goes oh my God what do I do.

And then you could add a part where embarrassment is walking outside of tiredness's house, with the thing back in the cage, and then embarrassment is not paying attention, but the second he looks up he sees a bunch of black Vans and his boss walking around, and embarrassed jaw drops and he goes oh God I have to act natural here like I'm in control of the situation, even though embarrassment sees all the trash cans and all the lights shattered and all of the debris from him searching for this thing frantically, And then his boss makes eye contact with him then he snaps to attention and he tries to act natural while he goes hey boss how you doing yeah it's all good got the thing here nothing to be afraid of hehe.

And the boss considers firing embarrassment after his dumb excuses but decides he still has some use to the science corporation since they know embarrassment knows tiredness but they don't tell him that.

And then add a scene where the science corporation is dismissing and invalidating embarrassment and is hanging the fact that they will fire him over his head in order to get him to do something shady without tiredness knowing, maybe put a tracker in his room or something because they want to be monitoring tiredness because they know tiredness interacted with the creature.

And then add a scene where it's not a tracker but it's a recorder because they want to record what is going on with tiredness, and so while embarrassment is interacting with tiredness, embarrassment is acting strange but he's trying to keep it cool because he doesn't want tiredness to let on, but embarrassment is also conflicted because he's trying to also make sure tiredness doesn't reveal too many secrets to this evil corporation that he doesn't like, and he's also trying to be nice to his friend, but he's also trying to make sure tiredness doesn't implicate himself or something, but he also knows the evil corporation is listening in because he's holding the recording device in his clothes.

And then you could have a scene where tiredness and embarrassment are interacting, and then embarrassment trips or something and then they're recording device falls out of his pocket, and embarrassment is trying to cover up what just happened, but tiredness is on to him now, but tiredness knows now that embarrassment has the recording device and knows someone is watching them, but he doesn't want to let embarrassment know that he knows, so tiredness pretends he accept embarrassment excuses, and now tiredness is trying to figure out what kind of recording of device it was and why this corporation that embarrassment is working for wanted to record him.

You could have a scene where tiredness after researching the corporation, has decided to infiltrate the lab himself, and look around, but he's not going to go in like ninja style, but he's going to use his social engineering and deadpan humor and personality to have the security and the people either think he's not a threat or just let him through cuz he's such a boring looking person, but then he gets into the research lab and sees what this creature was and he trips an alarm, and he's running away from the guards chasing him and then he smashes through the window and thinks he's going to die because he's falling to the ground, but then his powers kick in right when he's about to think that he's going to die.
